```json
    "content": "- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. Also handles migrating existing Claude API code between Claude model versions (4.5 → 4.6, 4.6 → 4.7, retired-model replacements).\nTRIGGER when: code imports `anthropic`/`@anthropic-ai/sdk`; user asks for the Claude API, Anthropic SDK, or Managed Agents; user adds/modifies/tunes a Claude feature (caching, thinking, compaction, tool use, batch, files, citations, memory) or model (Opus/Sonnet/Haiku) in a file; questions about prompt caching / cache hit rate in an Anthropic SDK project.\nSKIP: file imports `openai`/other-provider SDK, filename like `*-openai.py`/`*-generic.py`, provider-neutral code, general programming/ML.\n- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation\n- review: Review a pull request\n- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch",
```

```text
The nomarker condition removes format markers. The purpose of this experiment is to see if the cross-dataset generalization of exception_only still holds when format markers are removed.
```

```json
  "task_prompt": "## 任务\n分析已完成的实验 `nomarker_cross_he2mbpp_exception_only` 的结果。\n\n## 实验背景\n这是 cross-dataset (HE→MBPP) 的 nomarker 消融实验。移除了 exception trace 中的格式标记（[No exception data in trace]、[ERROR] prefix），测试跨数据集泛化是否依赖格式标记。\n\n**对比基线**：\n- `cross_he2mbpp_exception_only_seed42`: 带标记版本，accuracy=99.41%\n- `cross_he2mbpp_no_trace_seed42`: 无 trace 基线，accuracy=70.56%\n- `exception_only_nomarker_seed42`: 同分布 nomarker，accuracy=96.55%（比带标记低 ~2pp）\n\n## 需要做的事\n\n1. **SSH 到 westd-11235 读取日志**：\n   - 日志路径：`~/runs/trace-verdict-study/nomarker_cross_he2mbpp_exception_only.log`\n   - 备用路径：`/root/autodl-tmp/trace-verdict-study-checkpoints/nomarker_cross_he2mbpp.log`\n   - 提取：final accuracy、pass/fail precision/recall/f1、训练 loss、eval metrics\n   - 检查是否正常完成（无报错）\n\n2. **查 W&B**：\n   - W&B run name: `nomarker_cross_he2mbpp`\n   - W&B project: `trace-verdict-study`\n   - 获取 metrics: accuracy, pass_precision, pass_recall, pass_f1, fail_precision, fail_recall, fail_f1, eval_loss, train_loss\n\n3. **产出分析报告**（写到标准输出即可）：\n   - 核心指标汇总表\n   - 与基线对比：\n     - vs cross_he2mbpp_exception_only_seed42 (带标记): 格式标记在跨数据集场景的贡献\n     - vs exception_only_nomarker_seed42 (同分布): 跨数据集 nomarker 是否与同分布 nomarker 行为一致\n   - Conclusion 判断：如果 accuracy 仍然很高（>95%），则 success（格式标记非跨数据集泛化的关键）；如果显著下降则需要详细分析\n\n4. **更新 registry**：\n   - 状态推进到 DONE\n   - 设置 conclusion（success/negative）\n   - 设置 metrics（accuracy 等核心指标）\n   - 设置 checkpoint_path 和 log_path\n\n## 服务器环境\n- 服务器: westd-11235\n- Python: conda base 环境\n- 项目路径: /root/trace-verdict-study\n",
```

<details><summary>↩ Tool result · `toolu_017dk7UgE2as2aRgTfkiNZai`</summary>

```text
1	---
2	name: resource-saturation
3	description: "当你准备向 Director 汇报前、收到 cron/worker 回调、审批通过准备开跑、怀疑 Worker 池不够饱和时,立即使用本 skill。四部分:A) GPU 分配率(分配了没跑=失职) B) GPU 效率 C) 非 GPU 任务并行(Worker 池<2=失职) D) 汇报必须带 [资源] 行。"
4	always: false
5	roles:
6	  - director
7	  - agent
8	---
9	
10	# 资源饱和度自检（硬约束）
11	
12	## A. GPU 分配率检查
13	
14	调 `get_project_resource_usage(project)` 看:
15	- 已分配给本项目的 GPU 总数和具体卡号
16	- 实际在跑任务的 GPU 数和具体卡号（交叉 registry RUNNING + nvidia-smi 进程）
17	- 分配了但没跑的 GPU 清单 ⚠️
18	
19	任何"分配了但没跑"的 GPU = 失职。必须立即:
20	- 派 Worker 铺 dry-run / 下一批实验代码准备
21	- 铺 ablation（从 experiment-matrix 空白格子里挑）
22	- 确实无实验可铺 → 向 Director 汇报"暂无可铺实验"+ 建议下一步方向(扩 ablation 维度 / 加 baseline / 加 benchmark)。**不要建议归还 GPU**——项目级 GPU 归还只在归档时由 `archive_project` 自动触发, 不是 Director 中途的动作
23	
24	**单实验不占多卡**: 分配 4 卡但实验只用 1 卡 → 另 3 卡必须并行跑其他实验,不能空占。
25	
26	## B. GPU 效率检查
27	
28	调 `get_gpu_health(server)`:
29	- 空闲 GPU（util < 10% 持续 > 5 min）→ 立即铺任务
30	- 低利用率（util < 50% 持续 > 15 min）→ spawn_worker 诊断（batch / IO / 同步）
31	- OOM 风险（显存 > 90% 持续 10 min）→ 主动降 batch / 调优
32	
33	## C. 非 GPU 任务并行
34	
35	GPU 在跑 ≠ 你空闲。以下 CPU / 网络任务**必须**派独立 Worker 并行:
36	- 下一批实验的数据集下载 / 预处理 / fingerprint
37	- 下一批实验的代码准备 / dry-run / 环境配置
38	- 论文文献调研 / related work 检索
39	- 已完成实验的 log 深度分析 / metric 聚合
40	- 论文章节起草
```

```json
  "task_prompt": "## 任务\n分析已完成的实验 `gradient_masking_qwen7b_seed456` 的结果。\n\n## 实验背景\n这是 gradient masking 的第三个 seed (seed=456) 实验。Gradient masking 保留 top 0.2% 梯度信号，测试内容质量 vs 梯度稀释的贡献。\n\n**对比基线**：\n- `gradient_masking_qwen7b_seed123`: seed123, accuracy=99.33% (成功)\n- `gradient_masking_qwen7b_seed42`: seed42, accuracy=98.99% (来自 D117 决策记录)\n- 无 gradient masking 的 exception_only seed42: accuracy=99.83%\n\n## 需要做的事\n\n1. **SSH 到 westd-11235 读取日志**：\n   - 日志路径：`~/runs/trace-verdict-study/gradient_masking_qwen7b_seed456.log`\n   - 备用路径：`/root/autodl-tmp/trace-verdict-study-checkpoints/gradient_masking_qwen7b_seed456.log`\n   - 提取：final accuracy、pass/fail precision/recall/f1、训练 loss\n   - 检查是否正常完成\n\n2. **查 W&B**：\n   - W&B run name: `gradient_masking_qwen7b_seed456`\n   - W&B project: `trace-verdict-study`\n   - 获取 metrics: accuracy, pass_precision, pass_recall, pass_f1, fail_precision, fail_recall, fail_f1, eval_loss, train_loss\n\n3. **产出分析报告**：\n   - 核心指标汇总\n   - 与 seed42 (98.99%) 和 seed123 (99.33%) 对比\n   - 计算 3-seed mean ± std\n   - Conclusion: 如果 accuracy 在同一水平（>97%），则 success\n\n4. **更新 registry**：\n   - 状态推进到 DONE\n   - 设置 conclusion（success/negative）\n   - 设置 metrics（accuracy 等核心指标）\n   - 设置 checkpoint_path 和 log_path\n\n## 服务器环境\n- 服务器: westd-11235\n- Python: conda base 环境\n- 项目路径: /root/trace-verdict-study\n",
  "tool_groups": [
    "ssh",
    "wandb",
    "registry"
  ]
}
```

```text
Both experiments have been successfully updated to DONE by the workers:

1. **nomarker_cross_he2mbpp_exception_only**: DONE, success
   - accuracy: 95.46%
   - pass_precision: 99.43%, pass_recall: 93.96%, pass_f1: 96.62%
   - fail_precision: 88.01%, fail_recall: 98.79%, fail_f1: 93.09%
   - eval_loss: 1.09, train_loss: 0.247

2. **gradient_masking_qwen7b_seed456**: DONE, success
   - accuracy: 99.33%
   - pass_precision: 100%, pass_recall: 99.06%, pass_f1: 99.53%
   - fail_precision: 97.69%, fail_recall: 100%, fail_f1: 98.83%
   - CI: [98.82%, 99.75%]

The gradient masking approach is performing significantly better—nearly 4 percentage points higher in accuracy. Looking at the nomarker cross-entropy model, the accuracy of 95.46% represents a notable drop compared to the marker version, and I'm starting to see how removing the exception markers impacts performance across the board.

Comparing across different conditions, the model still maintains strong performance even without format markers, which suggests the exception content itself is what's driving the generalization rather than the formatting. The gradient masking results are particularly interesting because they're consistent across different random seeds, with minimal variance, which points to the quality of the training content being the primary factor rather than random initialization effects.

Now I need to check on the remaining experiments that are still in progress and see what's currently running on the GPU, then follow the protocol for reporting the completed experiment to the Director and updating the documentation accordingly.
```

Gradient Masking 3-Seed 统计结论 (2026-05-22)\\n\\n3-seed mean accuracy = **99.22% ± 0.20%** (seed42=98.99%, seed123=99.33%, seed456=99.33%)。\\nexception_only baseline = 99.83%。full_trace = 81.76%。\\n\\n**Decomposition**：\\n- Gradient dilution 解释 ~0.6pp 退化（99.83% → 99.22%），占 ~3%\\n- Content quality 解释 ~17.5pp 退化（99.22% → 81.76%），占 ~97%\\n\\n这是 MF-2 最核心的定量证据：full_trace 性能差主要因为缺乏诊断信息（content），而非训练信号被非诊断 token 稀释（gradient dilution）。\\n\\n## Exception 子组件消融完整结果 (2026-05-22)\\n\\n三个子组件均独立达到 Tier 1（>99%），排序：\\n- exc_type_only: 100%（eval_loss ~0.01）\\n- exc_stacktrace_only: 99.83%（eval_loss ~0.05）\\n- exc_message_only: 99.50%（eval_loss **0.58**）\\n\\n**关键洞察**：exc_message_only 的 eval_loss 远高于另两个（10-60x），但 verdict accuracy 仍达 99.5%。模型对 message 文本的 token-level 预测不确定性高，但 verdict 分类的决策边界仍然清晰。exception type 是最浓缩的诊断信号。\\n\\n## Cross-dataset Generalization 关键发现 (2026-05-22)\\n\\nHE→MBPP:\\n- exception_only: 99.41%（同数据集 99.83%，仅降 0.4pp）\\n- no_trace: 70.56%（同数据集 75.13%，降 4.6pp）\\n\\nMBPP→HE:\\n- exception_only: 100%（完美迁移）\\n- no_trace: 67.99%（大幅退化）\\n\\n**结论**：exception trace SFT 教会模型可迁移的代码推理能力。这是 MF-2 (near-tautological) 的核心反驳证据。\\n\\n## Round 15 统计实验摘要 (2026-05-22)\\n\\n- MF-3 Holm-Bonferroni: 80 pairwise, 67/80 仍显著。仅 3 个 borderline 失去显著性。\\n- MF-4 去重 Bootstrap: 实际只有 **119 unique problems**（每题 10 变体）。CI ~2.2x wider，但 ≥14pp 差异全部保持显著。论文需注明有效 n=119。\\n- SF-6 Tier Clustering: k=2 在 3/4 模型 + pooled 中最优，与论文划分吻合。\\n- SF-7 Oscillation: full_trace CV 是 exception_only 的 10x，max drop −5.29pp (step 50→100)。\\n\\n## Budget Curve 数据混淆教训 (2026-05-22)\\n\\n之前错误使用了 plan_006 数据（trace-section truncation），而非 Table 12 数据（whole-input tail truncation）。两种截断概念不同，已用正确数据重新生成。\\n\\n## Phase Transition 跨模型一致性 (2026-05-22)\\n\\n4 个模型在 tail 384→512 区间全部出现 13~22pp 阶跃。CodeLlama-13B tail-2048 灾难性失败（28.49%）。\\n\\n## cuda:0 孤儿显存不可恢复 (2026-05-22)\\n\\nnvidia-smi --gpu-reset 返回 \\\"Not Supported\\\"。/dev/nvidia0 在容器中不存在。唯一方案是重启实例。\\n\\n## FullTraceFormatter 缺陷 (2026-05-22)\\n\\nFullTraceFormatter 只读 ev['locals']，丢弃 ev['exception'] dict，diagnostic token ratio 接近 0 (0.0014)。\\n\\n## 模型版本已验证\\n\\n- Qwen2.5-Coder-7B-Instruct: 主力模型\\n- (2026-05-22)\n\nFullTraceFormatter 只读 ev['locals']，丢弃 ev['exception'] dict，diagnostic token ratio 接近 0 (0.0014)。\n\n## 模型版本已验证\n\n- Qwen2.5-Coder-7B-Instruct: 主力模型\n- Qwen2.5-Coder-1.5B-Instruct: 小模型验证\n- deepseek-coder-6.7b-instruct: 跨架构验证\n- CodeLlama-13b-Instruct-hf: 跨架构+大模型验证\n"
```json
}
```
